Abstract: Superconducting circuits exhibit remarkable potential for constructing large-scale quantum simulation and computation systems, featuring numerous qubits, extended coherence time, and precise control. Nevertheless, the growing number of signal cables poses a challenge in dilution refrigerators due to space and heat load constraints. To overcome this issue, we experimentally implemented an optically-assisted transmission line as an alternative to coaxial cables. By modulating microwave signals on laser intensities at room temperature and regenerating the signals at a cryogenic plate within the dilution refrigerator, we demonstrated full control of superconducting qubits using photocurrent. We demonstrate and benchmark both single-qubit and two-qubit gates on frequency tunable transmon qubits, achieving fidelities of 99.915% $\pm$ 0.005% and 99.676% $\pm$ 0.041%, respectively, which have reached the requirement of the surface code.

</details>


### [115] [Variational Quantum Linear Solver via Block Encoding for the Poisson Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19655)
*Viraj Dsouza,Ayush Singhal,Alex Khan,Rut Lineswala,Abhishek Chopra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于精确块编码离散拉普拉斯算子的变分量子线性求解器（VQLS）用于求解泊松方程，与LCU-based方法需要O(L²)个不同电路不同，该方法仅需单个电路即可完成代价函数评估，并在多个物理基准问题上验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于LCU的VQLS方法在每次代价函数评估时需要O(L²)个不同的电路，其中L是离散拉普拉斯算子LCU分解中的项数，这导致计算开销随系统规模增大而急剧增加，限制了VQLS的实用性。

Method: 该方法采用离散拉普拉斯算子的精确块编码构建VQLS，使得代价函数评估仅需单个电路而非O(L²)个电路。此外，论文实证研究了经典优化器的选择对变分优化进展停滞的影响，并在三个物理基准问题上进行测试：正弦强迫项泊松方程、带局域高斯源的稳态热传导问题（均为Dirichlet边界条件），以及二维顶盖驱动空腔流的压力-泊松方程（Neumann边界条件，每时间步调用一次求解器）。

Result: 在物理驱动的基准测试中，该VQLS方法成功求解了上述三个问题，且相比LCU-based方法大幅降低了电路数量需求。同时发现经典优化器的选择显著影响变分优化的收敛特性。

Conclusion: 基于精确块编码的VQLS是一种比LCU-based方法更高效的泊松方程量子求解方案，电路资源需求显著降低，适用于包括复杂流体模拟在内的多类物理问题。

Abstract: We present a variational quantum linear solver (VQLS) for the Poisson equation built on an exact block encoding of the discrete Laplacian, and demonstrate its performance on physically motivated benchmarks. Unlike LCU-based VQLS where the number of distinct circuits required per cost-function evaluation is $\mathcal{O}(L^2)$, where $L$ is the number of terms in the LCU decomposition of the discrete Laplacian operator, this approach requires only a single circuit for cost evaluation. We further empirically demonstrate that the choice of classical optimizer materially affects where the variational optimization ceases to make progress. The solver is benchmarked on three problems: a Poisson equation with sinusoidal forcing and a steady-state heat conduction problem with a localized Gaussian source, both with Dirichlet boundaries, and the pressure-Poisson equation of a two-dimensional lid-driven cavity flow, in which the solver is invoked once per time step under Neumann boundary conditions.

</details>


### [116] [Tight Entropy Contraction of Generalized Quantum Depolarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19685)
*Li Gao,Long Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为广义量子去极化信道和半群建立了相对熵收缩的上下界，给出了收缩率关于维度常数的一阶渐近紧界。上界估计基于尖逆向比和相对熵凸性（可由Hockey-Stick量子f散度及Bogoliubov--Kubo--Mori量子Fisher信息度量推导），下界则来自一般条件期望下存在达到指标的纯态。结果推广至完全熵收缩率与张量稳定的乘积动力学估计，涵盖量子去极化、退相和紧群对称化，并影响相干性和不对称性的衰减速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对熵收缩是量子信息论中刻画信道信息耗散的关键量，但其精确速率界限在广义量子去极化信道中尚未完善。作者旨在建立与维度常数匹配的紧致上下界，并实现张量稳定（tensor stable）的估计，以覆盖更广泛的量子动力学过程。

Method: 上界方面，利用尖逆向比（sharp reverse ratio）与相对熵凸性，这些可由Hockey-Stick量子f散度以及Bogoliubov--Kubo--Mori量子Fisher信息度量分别独立推导获得。下界方面，利用一般条件期望下存在达到收缩指标的纯态这一事实来构造最优下界。通过上述技术路线，建立了维度常数意义下一阶渐近紧的收缩率界，并将其推广到完全收缩率和乘积动力学的张量稳定估计。

Result: 获得了广义量子去极化信道与半群的相对熵收缩的匹配上下界，收缩率按维度常数取得紧的一阶渐近形式；相关结果扩展到完全熵收缩率和张量稳定估计，并应用于量子去极化、退相和紧群对称化过程，给出了相干性与不对称性衰减速率的具体推论。

Conclusion: 本文在广义量子去极化信道的相对熵收缩问题上取得了维度最优的紧致界限，方法上融合了量子f散度与量子Fisher信息度量两种独立途径，且结果具有张量稳定性，为量子耗散动力学中的相干性和不对称性衰减提供了定量的理论工具。

Abstract: We establish upper and lower bounds for relative entropy contraction of generalized quantum depolarizing channels and semigroups. Our bound provides tight first order asymptotic of the contraction rate in terms of the dimension constant. One side estimate are based on sharp reverse ratio and convexity of relative entropy of two states, which can be derived from the recently introduced Hockey-Stick quantum $f$-divergence, and also independently, Bogoliubov--Kubo--Mori quantum Fisher information metric. The other side follows from the existence of index achieving pure state with respect to a general conditional expectation. Our results extend to the complete entropy contraction rate, tensor stable estimates for product dynamics. Examples include quantum depolarization, dephasing, and compact group symmetrization, with consequences for the decay rate of coherence and asymmetry.

</details>


### [117] [Multiplexing of Continuous-Variable and Discrete-Variable Quantum Key Distribution Systems over Fibered and Free-Space Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19745)
*Mattia Sabatini,Edoardo Rossi,Matías R. Bolaños,Francesco Vedovato,Thomas Liege,Eleni Diamanti,Giuseppe Vallone,Paolo Villoresi,Yoann Piétri,Marco Avesani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次实验演示了在共享光信道上同时运行CV-QKD和DV-QKD两个独立系统，通过标准C波段DWDM滤波器在光纤和620米城区日光自由空间链路上实现共存，无测量到的多路复用串扰代价，确立了混合CV-DV WDM作为异构量子通信网络的实用构建模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来量子通信基础设施需在同一物理信道上服务异构用户：短距离高吞吐场景适合CV-QKD，长距离高损耗场景适合DV-QKD。虽然WDM可实现两者共存，但由于二者噪声敏感性差异显著，共存极具挑战且此前从未实验验证。

Method: 利用标准C波段DWDM滤波器（CV在1550.12 nm，DV在1545.32 nm），在共享光信道上同时运行两个独立的CV-QKD和DV-QKD系统，并在光纤和620 m城区日光自由空间链路上进行联合操作实验验证。

Result: 在光纤上，两系统呈现预期的互补性，在7.56 dB信道损耗处交叉，二者均达到约1.43 Mbit/s密钥率；在日光自由空间链路中，在时变大气衰减下双方均维持Mbit/s级密钥率。所有配置下均未观察到可测量的QBER或额外噪声的多路复用代价。

Conclusion: 混合CV-DV WDM被确立为异构量子通信网络的实用构建模块，使城域高吞吐用户与长距离骨干链路能够在单一物理基础设施上被同时服务。

Abstract: Future quantum communication infrastructures will need to serve heterogeneous users on shared physical channels: short-range, high-throughput links favor Continuous-Variable Quantum Key Distribution (CV-QKD), while long-reach, high-loss links remain the domain of Discrete-Variable QKD (DV-QKD). Wavelength-division multiplexing (WDM) of the two protocols on a common channel would address both regimes simultaneously, but their markedly different noise sensitivities make coexistence non-trivial and, to date, experimentally untested. Here we report the first simultaneous operation of two independent CV- and DV-QKD systems on a common optical channel, using standard C-band DWDM filters at 1550.12 nm (CV) and 1545.32 nm (DV). We demonstrate joint operation on both optical fiber and a 620 m urban daylight free-space link. On fiber, the two systems exhibit the expected complementarity, crossing over at 7.56 dB of channel loss where both deliver $\sim$1.43 Mbit/s; in daylight free-space, both sustain Mbit/s key rates under time-varying atmospheric attenuation. Across all configurations we observe no measurable multiplexing-induced penalty in QBER or excess noise. These results establish hybrid CV-DV WDM as a practical building block for heterogeneous quantum communication networks, where metropolitan high-throughput users and long-reach backbone links can be served on a single physical infrastructure.

</details>


### [118] [An Irreducible Quantum Advantage in Aligning World Models with Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19779)
*Josep Lumbreras,Hailan Ma,Jayne Thompson,Mile Gu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明经典世界模型无法通过增加记忆容量来完美模拟某些经典真实世界（其最优策略总会在某条轨迹上失配），但单个量子比特（qutrit）构成的量子世界模型可以精确复现这些世界，使真实世界与虚拟世界的最优策略完美对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型是智能体在真实世界部署前进行训练和测试的关键工具。其核心目标是让虚拟世界中的最优策略与真实世界中的最优策略对齐。一个自然的假设是：只要增加模型的记忆容量，就能构建足够精确的模型。作者旨在检验这一假设是否成立。

Method: 作者构造了一类"真实世界"，并证明对于任意有限记忆的经典世界模型，都存在至少一条可能轨迹使其失败：要么失去区分行动的能力（当真实世界明显偏好某个行动时），要么反复将最高期望奖励赋给次优行动，且其期望奖励估计保持非零平均误差。作为对比，作者展示了这些真实世界可以用单个qutrit作为状态空间的量子世界模型精确复现，且奖励估计和偏好行动始终与真实世界一致。

Result: 经典世界模型的局限性被严格证明：即使真实世界本身是经典的，有限记忆的经典模型也无法保证策略对齐。而量子世界模型（仅用单个qutrit）能够精确再现这些世界，实现奖励估计和行动偏好的完美匹配，从而确保真实与虚拟世界的最优策略完全对齐。

Conclusion: 经典世界模型在记忆能力上存在根本性局限，增加记忆无法克服这一障碍；而量子世界模型展现出超越经典的模拟能力，即使使用极小的量子状态空间（单个qutrit）也能完美模拟经典世界，为构建更可靠的世界模型提供了新方向。

Abstract: World models provide digital simulacra of the true world, allowing agents to be trained and tested before costly real-world deployment. At each time step, they receive an action and generate an observation and reward matching the statistics of the true world. In complex environments where present outcomes depend on events far in the past, this requires memory. One might expect that, by increasing memory, we can always build a model accurately enough to align the optimal agent policies of the real and virtual worlds. We show that this is false for classical world models, even when the true world itself is classical. We construct true worlds for which every finite classical model fails along the same possible trajectory: it either loses the ability to distinguish actions when the true world clearly prefers one, or repeatedly assigns the highest expected reward to suboptimal actions. Its expected-reward estimates also retain a nonvanishing average error. In contrast, each such true world admits a quantum world model using a single qutrit that reproduces it exactly: its reward estimates and preferred actions always match those of the true world, ensuring that the optimal policies of the real and virtual worlds remain perfectly aligned.

</details>


### [119] [Constant-round quantum advantage in communication complexity for total functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19787)
*Atsuya Hasegawa,François Le Gall*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了存在一个全函数（total function），其随机通信复杂度与常数轮量子通信复杂度之间存在多项式差距（polynomial gap）。此前这种分离仅在对量子协议使用多项式轮数时已知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已知随机通信与量子通信之间存在多项式分离，但仅限于量子协议使用多项式轮数的情况。本文旨在探索在更受限的常数轮量子协议下是否仍能保持这种分离，以深化对量子通信复杂度优势边界的理解。

Method: 构造了一个具体的全函数，并证明其随机通信复杂度与常数轮量子通信复杂度之间存在多项式差距，即在常数轮量子协议下，量子通信仍能实现显著优势。

Result: 证明了存在一个全函数，使得随机通信复杂度与常数轮量子通信复杂度之间存在多项式级别的分离，将此前仅对多项式轮量子协议成立的分离结果推广到了常数轮情形。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，即使将量子协议限制在常数轮数，量子通信相比随机通信仍可保持多项式级别的优势，进一步刻画了量子通信在通信复杂度中的能力边界。

Abstract: We show that there exists a total function for which there is a polynomial gap between the randomized and the constant-round quantum communication complexity. Previously, such a separation was known only for quantum protocols using polynomially many rounds.

</details>


### [120] [Optomechanically induced transparency in the presence of a strong Duffing nonlinearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19811)
*Rong Zhang,Jing Qiu,Stefano Chesi,Yingdan Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Duffing非线性和光力学相互作用对腔态密度（DOS）的联合效应，该效应可通过光力学诱导透明（OMIT）实验直接观测。两种非线性引入相同的极化激元间相互作用类型，在光学响应中产生独特特征。作者推导了增强极化激元间非线性散射的共振条件，并详细研究了腔DOS的参数依赖性，从而表征了在OMIT实验中观测Duffing非线性量子效应的典型强度和最优条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Duffing非线性和光机械耦合在腔量子电动力学系统中的联合效应，理解两种非线性如何共同影响腔态密度，并为在OMIT实验中观测Duffing非线性的量子效应提供理论依据和参数指导。

Method: 通过理论推导建立包含Duffing非线性和光机械相互作用的模型，从系统哈密顿量出发分析极化激元间的非线性散射机制，推导增强非线性散射的共振条件，并对腔态密度进行系统的参数依赖研究。

Result: 发现两种非线性引入相同的极化激元间相互作用类型，在光学响应中产生可区分的特征；确定了增强非线性散射的共振条件；通过参数扫描表征了Duffing非线性量子效应的典型强度与最优观测条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为在OMIT实验中直接观测Duffing非线性的量子效应提供了理论预测和参数优化方案，明确了实现可观测量子效应的最佳实验条件。

Abstract: We consider the combined effect of a Duffing nonlinearity and the optomechanical interaction on the cavity density of states (DOS), which is directly observable in optomechanically induced transparency (OMIT) experiments. Both nonlinearities introduce the same type of interaction between polaritons, producing a distinct feature in the optical response. We derive the resonant condition that enhances the nonlinear scattering between polaritons and study the parameter dependence of the cavity DOS in detail. This allows us to characterize the typical strength and optimal conditions for observing the quantum effects of the Duffing nonlinearity in OMIT experiments.

</details>


### [121] [Distributed Variational Quantum Eigensolver: Embarrassingly Parallel strategies on NISQ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19824)
*Marta Losada,Daniel Faílde,Andrés Gómez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在NISQ设备上对变分量子特征求解器（VQE）进行分布式并行化的三种策略，并利用CUNQA平台在虚拟QPU上评估了异构噪声对并行化效率和精度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQE需要大量电路执行，非常适合分布式并行化以加速计算；但NISQ设备存在异构噪声，可能影响并行化结果的准确性和效率，因此需要系统评估不同并行化策略在噪声环境下的表现。

Method: 使用CUNQA平台模拟虚拟QPU，评估三种完全可并行化（embarrassingly parallel）策略：1) shot-level并行化（采样级别）；2) circuit-level并行化（针对梯度和可观测量）；3) candidate-level并行化（针对基于种群的优化器）。从加速比（speedup）和精度（accuracy）两个维度进行度量。

Result: 在CUNQA平台上对三种并行化策略进行了系统评估，考察了异构噪声下的加速比和精度表现（具体数值结果未在摘要中给出）。

Conclusion: 三种并行化策略各有适用场景，异构噪声会同时影响并行化的效率和结果精度，CUNQA平台可作为评估此类分布式量子计算策略的有效工具。

Abstract: Variational Quantum Eigensolver requires many circuit executions, making it ideal for distributed parallelization. However, heterogeneous noise in NISQ devices can skew results and efficiency. Using the CUNQA platform for emulation of virtual QPUs, we evaluate three embarrassingly parallelization strategies (shot-level, circuit-level for gradients and observables and candidate level for population-based optimizers) across metrics like speedup and accuracy.

</details>


### [122] [Disassembling qLDPC codes for depth-optimal parity-check circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19917)
*Minh T. P. Nguyen,Maximilian Rimbach-Russ,Stefano Bosco*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用qLDPC码Tanner图对称性来高效设计综合征提取电路的新方法，不需从整个量子码出发，而是从底层组件构建电路。该方法在Lifted Product和Balanced Product码上实现了可证明最优或接近最优的CNOT深度，在Quantum Tanner码上测试的所有实例（包括近600数据量子比特的码）均达到深度最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qLDPC码是实现可扩展容错量子计算的有前景路径，但其实际实现需要高效的综合征提取电路。许多qLDPC码族通过显式构造从小组件组装而成，其Tanner图带有边对称性，这些对称性可以用于简化电路设计。

Method: 利用qLDPC码构造中Tanner图固有的边对称性，从底层组件（而非完整量子码）设计综合征提取电路。对于Lifted Product和Balanced Product码，该方法提供了解析构造；对于Quantum Tanner码，采用类似思路生成深度最优的电路。

Result: Lifted Product和Balanced Product码上获得可证明最优或近最优的CNOT深度；Quantum Tanner码在所有测试实例上均达到深度最优，包括近600个数据量子比特的码。

Conclusion: 利用码构造中的对称性可以显著简化qLDPC码综合征提取电路的设计，使得电路设计从完整量子码层面降低到组件层面，在多个码族上实现最优或接近最优的电路深度。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes offer a promising route to scalable fault-tolerant quantum computing, but their practical implementation requires efficient circuits for syndrome extraction. Many qLDPC families are assembled from a small set of components through explicit constructions that imprint edge symmetries on their Tanner graphs. We show that these symmetries can be exploited to design syndrome-extraction circuits from the underlying components, rather than from the full quantum code. For Lifted Product and Balanced Product codes this approach yields an analytical construction with provably optimal or near-optimal CNOT depth. For Quantum Tanner codes it produces depth-optimal circuits on every instance we test, including codes up to nearly 600 data qubits.

</details>


### [123] [Entangling two qudits of arbitrary dimension through light-atomic Faraday interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19945)
*R. Surmay,V. A. Leonov,E. A. Vashukevich*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究任意维度的双量子位（qudit）纠缠操作，其中一个量子位编码在空间多模光场的轨道角动量（OAM）态中，另一个编码在原子集体自旋相干态的OAM中。通过在两次法拉第相互作用之间插入原子和光正交分量的旋转，实现了多种纠缠门，所有门均可表示为d维SWAP^α_d门的有理幂次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在扩展基于OAM的高维量子纠缠操作，探索利用光-原子混合系统生成通用且可调控的任意维度量子门，为高维量子信息处理提供理论框架。

Method: 采用包含两次法拉第相互作用及中间原子-光正交旋转的协议，系统表征所生成的可控酉操作族，并将其统一映射为SWAP^α_d门的幂次形式；同时计算不同逻辑空间维度和不同纠缠功率下双量子位变换的概率。

Result: 成功展示了一整类纠缠操作的生成能力，证明所有生成的量子门均可表示为d维SWAP^α_d门的有理幂；并给出了不同维度逻辑空间及不同纠缠功率下双量子位变换概率的定量结果。

Conclusion: 该协议为在光-原子混合平台上实现可调控的高维纠缠门提供了一种通用且可扩展的方案，其门操作的统一数学表征有助于后续高维量子计算和量子通信的应用开发。

Abstract: The paper investigates entangling operations acting on two qudits of arbitrary dimension, with one encoded in the states of a spatially multimode optical field, the other in those of atomic collective spin coherence, both carrying orbital angular momentum (OAM). We demonstrate the generation of a wide range of entangling operations within a protocol consisting of two Faraday interactions and a rotation of atomic and light quadratures between them. All generated gates can be represented as rational powers of the d-dimensional gate $SWAP^α_d$. The probabilities of two-qudit transformations are calculated for various dimensions of the logical space and for different entangling powers of the logical gates.

</details>


### [124] [Wide-field mid- to long-wave infrared imaging with undetected photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20015)
*Vladimir Kornienko,Nathan Gemmell,Caiyi Liu,Asteria Chen,Chris Phillips,Rupert Oulton*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了利用非共线相位匹配的硫代镓银（AgGaS₂）晶体，在6-10 μm（1670-1000 cm⁻¹）波长范围内实现宽场"未探测光子量子成像"（QIUP）。通过相干探测与红外光子"未被探测"的结合，成像性能比背景受限红外光电探测（BLIP）极限好约100倍。在8 μm波长下，图像具有超过8000±100个可分辨元素，分辨率为297±5 μm，采集时间为10秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统中红外成像受限于红外探测器的性能瓶颈（如背景噪声、冷却需求），而量子成像与未探测光子技术可通过仅在可见光范围测量干涉来探测中红外物体，从而利用成熟的硅相机技术。作者旨在突破QIUP在中红外波长范围（6-10 μm）的成像能力和性能极限。

Method: 利用AgGaS₂晶体中的非共线相位匹配实现宽场QIUP，结合相干探测方案，使红外光子保持"未被探测"状态的同时在可见波段进行干涉测量，从而实现中红外物体成像。

Result: 在6-10 μm波长范围内成功实现宽场QIUP成像；成像性能比BLIP极限提升约100倍；在8 μm处获得超过8000±100个可分辨元素，分辨率297±5 μm，仅需10秒采集时间。

Conclusion: 该研究为快速、无背景噪声、室温工作的光谱选择性中红外成像铺平了道路，为红外波段成像提供了超越传统红外探测器限制的新途径。

Abstract: Quantum imaging with undetected photons (QIUP) allows an object to be probed at mid-infrared frequencies by only measuring interference in the visible range, thus leveraging silicon camera technology. We show that non-collinear phase-matching in a silver thiogallate (AgGaS$_2$) crystal enables wide-field QIUP in the wavelength range of 6-10 $μ$m (1670-1000 cm$^{-1}$). A combination of coherent detection and infrared photons being ``undetected'' enables imaging at ${\sim}$100 times better than the background-limited infrared photodetection (BLIP) limit. At 8 $μ$m, our images have over 8000 $\pm$ 100 resolvable elements with a 297 $\pm$ 5 $μ$m resolution, and 10 s acquisition time. Our results pave the way to fast, background-noise-free, room-temperature, spectrally-selective mid-infrared imaging.

</details>


### [125] [Lensing and enhanced single atom detection via a single-pixel nanostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20032)
*Ling-Xiao Wang,Lei Xu,Ai-Ping Liu,Guang-Jie Chen,Yuan-Hao Yang,Jia-Qi Wang,Xin-Biao Xu,Guang-Can Guo,Chang-Ling Zou,Guo-Yong Xiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并演示了一种基于单个纳米结构散射光在近场中产生相位梯度的纳米级透镜机制，利用光学波导上的单原子作为量子探针通过荧光采样近场强度进行验证，实现了4倍单原子探测效率增强，确立了片上纳米结构作为多功能量子光学平台的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纳米透镜技术面临结构复杂、难以集成到片上光子电路的问题，且缺乏非破坏性、高灵敏度的近场光学表征手段。作者旨在开发一种简单通用的纳米级聚焦机制，并利用量子探针实现非破坏性的近场表征，以推动混合纳米光子-原子量子系统的发展。

Method: 作者提出利用单个纳米结构散射光时产生的近场相位梯度来实现纳米透镜效应。实验上采用基底上的光学波导结构，将单个原子作为量子探针，通过检测原子荧光来采样近场强度分布，从而非破坏性地表征聚焦光场并评估原子-光子耦合效率。

Result: 实验验证了该纳米透镜机制的有效性，通过量子探针技术成功表征了近场聚焦光场，并观察到单原子探测效率提高了4倍。

Conclusion: 该工作表明片上纳米结构可作为多功能量子光学平台，实现光子路由、场局域化和增强原子-光子耦合，为捕获和操控单原子以及构建混合纳米光子-原子系统用于量子应用开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We propose and demonstrate a general mechanism for nanoscale lensing based on the phase gradient imposed by a single nanostructure scattering light in its near-field. We verify this effect using an optical waveguide on a substrate, with single atoms serving as quantum probes that sample the near-field intensity through their fluorescence. This quantum probing technique provides a unique, non-destructive approach to characterizing focused optical fields and reveals a 4-fold enhancement in single atom detection efficiency. This work establishes on-chip nanostructures as a multi-functional quantum optics platform that can efficiently route photons, localize fields, and enhance atom-photon coupling, offering new opportunities for trapping and manipulating single atoms and realizing hybrid nanophotonic-atomic systems for quantum applications.

</details>


### [126] [Radial Convex Geometry of Quantum States and Its Relation to Best Separable Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20050)
*Haonan Qiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从量子态的凸几何角度研究两体纠缠，定义了基于可分离态边界与量子态边界相对位置的几何纠缠空间量G(ρ)和相对纠缠度Q(ρ)，并将其应用于最优可分近似（BSA），推导出一般约束界和两量子比特情形的显式表达式，揭示了BSA、鲁棒性与量子态空间中纠缠区域之间的几何关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从凸几何的视角统一理解量子纠缠，将BSA、纠缠鲁棒性等概念与量子态空间的几何结构（可分离态边界与量子态边界的相对位置）联系起来，从而提供更直观的几何描述和普适的定量界。

Method: 以最大混合态为参考点，利用可分离边界与量子态边界的相对位置定义几何纠缠空间量G(ρ)=[1-L(ρ)]/L(ρ)，并引入相对纠缠度Q(ρ)=[(1-p)/p]/G(ρ)与最大混合态的鲁棒性进行比较。将此几何构造应用于BSA最优分解，推导了一般界(1-p)L_B/[p(1-L_B)]≤p_0≤Q(ρ)，并证明BSA纠缠分量的纠缠空间尺寸不小于原混合态。对两量子比特态，利用PPT判据显式评估几何参数，得到(1-p)/(2p)≤p_0≤Q(ρ)。

Result: 导出了BSA最优分解的一般约束不等式((1-p)L_B/[p(1-L_B)]≤p_0≤Q(ρ))，证明了BSA纠缠分量的纠缠空间尺寸单调性([1-L_R]/L_R≤[1-L_B]/L_B)；针对两量子比特态，BSA纠缠分量退化为纯纠缠态，并利用PPT判据获得了显式界(1-p)/(2p)≤p_0≤Q(ρ)。

Conclusion: 该几何框架为理解BSA、纠缠鲁棒性与量子态空间纠缠区域之间的关系提供了简洁统一的几何描述，表明纠缠分量在几何意义上不会小于原混合态的纠缠空间尺寸，且两量子比特情形下可精确量化这些界。

Abstract: We study bipartite entanglement from the convex geometry of quantum states. Taking the maximally mixed state as a reference point, we define a geometric entangled-space quantity $G(ρ)=[1-L(ρ)]/L(ρ)$ from the relative positions of the separable and quantum-state boundaries, and introduce a relative entanglement degree $Q(ρ)=[(1-p)/p]/G(ρ)$ by comparing it with the robustness relative to the maximally mixed state.
  We apply this geometric construction to the Best Separable Approximation (BSA). For the optimal decomposition, we derive the general bound $(1-p)L_B/[p(1-L_B)]\leq p_0\leq Q(ρ)$ and show that the entangled component of the BSA has an entangled-space size no smaller than that of the original mixed state, namely $[1-L_R]/L_R\leq[1-L_B]/L_B$.
  For two-qubit states, the BSA entangled component is a pure entangled state. Using the PPT criterion, its geometric parameter can be evaluated explicitly, giving the bound $(1-p)/(2p)\leq p_0\leq Q(ρ)$. These results provide a simple geometric description of the relation between BSA, robustness, and the entangled region of the quantum-state space.

</details>


### [127] [PPT Entanglement with Correlated Catalysis: Monotones and Irreversibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20063)
*Jingsong Ao,Aby Philip,Alexander Streltsov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子纠缠理论中带关联催化的状态变换下的基本限制，证明正则化PPT相对熵具有完全可加性和强超可加性，并表明即使允许任意关联催化剂，混合态纠缠操纵的不可逆性仍然无法消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子催化剂可以在不被消耗的情况下克服原本不可能实现的量子态变换，而允许催化剂与输出产生关联会使其辅助能力大幅增强。这引出了一个纠缠理论中的基本问题：当这种关联催化剂被自由提供时，状态操纵还受哪些限制制约？回答这一问题对于理解纠缠资源理论的边界至关重要。

Method: 在PPT（正部分转置）资源理论框架下，确定了正则化相对熵度量变得强超可加的一般条件，并利用这些条件构造了在不了解催化剂任何信息的情况下约束关联催化PPT变换的单调量。具体证明正则化PPT相对熵的完全可加性与强超可加性。

Result: 解决了纠缠理论中的一个开放问题——正则化PPT相对熵的完全可加性及强超可加性得到证明。更重要的是，这些约束表明即使任意关联催化剂也不能恢复渐近可逆性：对显式状态，最优纠缠蒸馏速率严格小于纠缠成本，说明混合态纠缠操纵的一些本质限制在催化剂帮助下仍然存在。

Conclusion: 即使提供实质性的催化辅助，混合态纠缠操纵中的某些基本限制仍然无法被克服，即关联催化无法使PPT资源理论恢复渐近可逆性。

Abstract: Quantum catalysts can overcome otherwise impossible quantum state transformations without being consumed, and allowing them to become correlated with the output makes this assistance substantially more powerful. This raises a fundamental question for entanglement theory: which limitations on state manipulation remain when such correlated catalysts are freely available? We answer this question in the positive-partial-transpose (PPT) resource theory, which allows a substantially broader class of operations than local operations and classical communication (LOCC). We identify general conditions under which regularized relative-entropy measures become strongly superadditive, and use them to construct monotones that constrain correlated catalytic PPT transformations without any knowledge of the catalyst. In particular, we prove that the regularized PPT relative entropy is fully additive and strongly superadditive, resolving an open problem in entanglement theory. Most importantly, these constraints show that even arbitrary correlated catalysts cannot restore asymptotic reversibility: for an explicit state, the optimal entanglement distillation rate remains strictly smaller than the entanglement cost. Thus, substantial catalytic assistance does not remove some of the fundamental limitations of mixed-state entanglement manipulation.

</details>


### [128] [Shadow models of a quantum model for cloud cover and the influence of finite sampling noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20076)
*Hedwig Keller,Mierk Schwabe,Veronika Eyring*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究如何通过构造性"影子模型"（无显式训练/回归阶段）将量子机器学习模型经典化，以便高效耦合到气候模型等经典应用中，并在Euro-Q-Exa量子系统上验证了该方法的降噪效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子计算硬件资源稀缺且量子-经典混合计算耦合困难，训练好的QML模型难以直接集成到经典应用中。作者希望在不使QML模型冗余的前提下，通过构造影子模型实现QML的经典化替代，从而高效地将量子资源限制在训练阶段。

Method: 采用构造性影子化过程，不涉及显式训练或回归阶段。具体方法包括：(1) 将量子电路表示为部分Fourier级数，利用离散Fourier变换在经典侧高效重建电路；(2) 通过截断部分Fourier级数进一步缩小影子模型的规模；(3) 将其应用于先前开发的云覆盖QML模型，并与经典插值方法进行了比较。

Result: 两种影子化方法在特定条件下都能减轻有限采样噪声。在Euro-Q-Exa（基于IQM Radiance超导量子比特系统）上计算影子模型时，也观察到了误差缓解效果，但难以将其与系统校准相关的误差区分开来。

Conclusion: 影子模型不仅解决了当前混合量子-经典计算中的耦合难题，还有望在量子硬件充足的时代之后继续发挥作用，因为它们本身就具有噪声缓解效果。

Abstract: Quantum computing is a quickly growing field that is promising various advantages compared to conventional computing. However, currently stand-alone quantum applications are scarce and hybrid (quantum-classical) computing is needed, especially in quantum machine learning (QML). Due to current limitations of quantum computing hardware and the coupling between HPC and quantum devices, integrating a trained (QML) model in classical applications is challenging. In this case, it is helpful to couple so-called shadows of the QML model instead, i.e., classical models that imitate the input-output relations of QML models such that quantum resources are only needed during the training stage. Here we consider constructive shadowing processes without an explicit training or regression stage to avoid rendering the QML model redundant, and apply them to a previously developed QML model for cloud cover [1] to allow for an efficient coupling to a climate model. We compare classical interpolation methods to an approximation of the quantum Fourier model, the representation of the circuit as a partial Fourier series. The encoding strategy in [1] allows the use of the discrete Fourier transform to efficiently reconstruct the circuits classically. Truncating the partial Fourier series further reduces the size of the shadow models. Both methods have the effect of mitigating finite sampling noise under certain conditions, which yields a motivation to use shadow models also beyond the era of limited hardware availability. Further, we compute the shadow models on the quantum system Euro-Q-Exa, based on the IQM Radiance system with superconducting qubits, where error mitigating effects can also be observed, albeit it is still difficult to distinguish them from errors connected to the calibration of the system.

</details>


### [129] [Squeezed- and coherent-state quantum key distribution over a deployed hybrid fibre-free-space channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20088)
*Dnan A. E. Hajomer,Huy Q. Nguyen,Ivan Derkach,Andreas B. Kidmose,Edoardo Rossi,Mattia Sabatini,Yoann Pietri,Marco Avesani,Francesco Vedovato,Michael Hentschel,Radim Filip,Giuseppe Vallone,Vladyslav Usenko,Tobias Gehring,Soren Forchhammer,Paolo Villoresi,Ulrik L. Andersen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文演示了在由620米自由空间链路和2公里部署光纤组成的混合信道上（总损耗高达20dB），实现本地生成本振的CV-QKD，同时支持高斯调制相干态和压缩态。通过统一的适应性后处理框架（包括基于透射率聚类的分簇、残余衰落缓解和速率自适应盲纠错），实现在混合光纤-自由空间信道上的密钥分发，分别获得0.42 Mbit/s（相干态）和0.93 Mbit/s（压缩态）的渐近密钥率，并将压缩态CV-QKD首次引入实际大气信道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络需要将光纤与自由空间链路相结合，但CV-QKD此前主要针对单一介质开发，在级联光纤-自由空间信道上的运行鲜有探索。光纤传输稳定且允许长处理间隔，而大气传播引入透射率波动，需在短时间尺度上解决，二者对系统提出了截然不同的要求，因此亟需验证CV-QKD在混合信道上的可行性。

Method: 将信道适配从光学层转移到后处理层，采用统一的适应性后处理框架，包含三个核心模块：基于透射率聚类的数据分簇、通过协方差矩阵平均或去衰落实现残差衰落缓解、以及速率自适应盲纠错。该框架同时应用于相干态和压缩态两种CV-QKD协议，并分别针对其安全分析和统计要求进行适配。

Result: 在620米自由空间+2公里光纤的混合信道上（总损耗达20dB），相干态协议获得0.42 Mbit/s的渐近密钥率，压缩态协议获得0.93 Mbit/s。适应性后处理框架单独可恢复高达19%的额外密钥，并在部署的大气信道上首次建立了压缩态CV-QKD。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对传输介质的适配可以在很大程度上转移到数据处理层，为覆盖光纤、地面自由空间和卫星链路的异构量子网络提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Quantum networks will combine optical fibre with free-space links, yet continuous-variable quantum key distribution (CV-QKD) has been developed predominantly for one medium or the other, while operation across concatenated fibre-free-space channels remains largely unexplored. The two media impose contrasting requirements: fibre transmission is stable and permits long processing intervals, whereas atmospheric propagation imposes transmittance fluctuations that degrade security and must be resolved on short timescales. Here we demonstrate a locally generated local oscillator CV-QKD with both Gaussian-modulated coherent and squeezed states over a deployed hybrid channel comprising a 620-m free-space link and 2 km of deployed fibre, with a total loss up to 20 dB. Rather than adapting the optics to each medium, we move channel adaptation to the post-processing, through a unified adaptive post-processing framework coupling transmittance-based clustering, residual-fading mitigation by covariance-matrix averaging or de-fading, and rate-adaptive blind reconciliation, which alone recovers up to 19% additional key. The same adaptive-processing principle is applied to both protocols, while accounting for their different security analyses and statistical requirements, yielding asymptotic secret-key rates of 0.42 Mbit per sec for the coherent-state protocol and 0.93 Mbit per sec for the squeezed-state protocol under the respective channel conditions, and establishing squeezed-state CV-QKD over a deployed atmospheric channel. These results show that adaptation to the transmission medium can largely be transferred to the data-processing layer, providing a route towards heterogeneous quantum networks spanning fibre, terrestrial free-space and satellite links.

</details>


### [130] [Counterexamples to the fractional coloring conjecture for triply efficient shadow tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20113)
*Jędrzej Stempin,Santiago Llorens,Felix Huber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文反驳了一个关于分数染色数与Pauli阴影层析的猜想。King等人提出的猜想声称，在给定量子态下具有非平凡期望值（≥ε）的Pauli算子的反对易图的分数色数为O(ε^{-2})。作者通过构造状态和可观测量族证明了该猜想为假，即不存在满足χ_f·ε²≤C的有限常数C。证明的关键工具是"放大技巧"，通过字典序图乘积放大分数色数、β数和期望值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数图染色在Pauli可观测量的阴影层析中具有重要应用价值。如果King等人提出的猜想为真，将对任意Pauli可观测量子集S存在三重高效的阴影层析算法。验证或反驳该猜想对于理解阴影层析的可行性和设计高效量子算法至关重要。

Method: 作者构造了一族特定量子态和可观测量，证明不存在满足χ_f·ε²≤C的有限常数C。此外还给出了一个更一般的构造，依赖于图的交换指标（commutation index）或β数。证明的核心技术是放大技巧：通过字典序图乘积（lexicographic graph products）同时放大分数色数、β数和期望值。

Result: 成功构造了反例族，证明King、Gosset、Kothari和Babbush论文中的猜想13（即χ_f·ε²有界）是错误的。这意味着不能仅基于该猜想推导出任意Pauli算子集合S的三重高效Pauli阴影层析算法。

Conclusion: 该猜想被证伪，表明分数色数与ε之间不存在如猜想所述的通用平方反比界。这为Pauli阴影层析算法的设计提出了新的挑战，需要寻找替代方法或更细致的结构性条件。

Abstract: Fractional graph colorings are useful for the Shadow tomography of Pauli observables. In practice, it is desirable that any experimentally interesting set of Pauli operators has a small fractional chromatic number $χ_{f}$ for its anticommutation graph. Conjecture 13 in King, Gosset, Kothari, and Babbush [PRX Quantum 6, 010336 (2025)] states that if $B_ε(\varrho)$ is the set of Pauli observables having expectation value magnitude at least $ε$ in some given quantum state $\varrho$, then the fractional chromatic number of the anticommutation graph $G$ induced by $B_ε(\varrho)$ is $O(ε^{-2})$. In other words, it asserts that there exists a constant $C$ such that $χ_{f} \cdot ε^2 \leq C$ on all states and graphs. If the conjecture were true, it would imply that there exists a triply efficient Pauli shadow tomography algorithm for {\it any} subset $S$ of Pauli observables, provided that there is also an efficient fractional coloring algorithm for the set $B_ε$. Here we show that the conjecture is false by constructing a family of states and observables for which no finite $C$ satisfying the bound exists. We also give a more general construction relying on the commutation index or $β$ number of a graph. The key ingredient in the proofs can be seen as an instance of the amplification trick, where fractional chromatic numbers, $β$ numbers, and expectation values are amplified through lexicographic graph products.

</details>


### [131] [SAKE: Spectral Autodiff Kernel Expansion for Geometric Liouvillian Transport. A Differential-Geometric Framework for Response Transport in Quantum Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20132)
*Eric R. Bittner,Carlos Silva-Acuna,Hao Li,Simon Paiva-Ortega*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SAKE（光谱自动微分核展开）的可微分计算框架，通过结合前向模式自动微分与Duhamel输运理论，在相邻量子动力学模型之间"输运"非线性光谱响应，而无需对每个哈密顿量或Liouvillian单独重新计算多维光谱。在具有su(2)×su(2)对称性的四能级激子二聚体上验证，三阶展开可精确复现投影输运算子及其路径混合效应，并揭示了相干和耗散微扰如何在双面Feynman路径间重新分配振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非线性光谱计算需要对每个参数化的哈密顿量或Liouvillian独立重新计算多维光谱，计算成本高昂，且在参数空间中缺乏连续性和可微性，难以支持高效的局部参数探索、灵敏度分析和逆向设计。

Method: SAKE框架结合两条核心思路：(1) 利用前向模式自动微分生成参数相关Liouvillian的一、二、三阶导数；(2) 利用Duhamel输运理论将这些导数组装成路径输运算子（pathway transport operator），该算子将参考模型的非线性响应映射到邻近系统上，实现多路径间的输运。

Result: 在具有su(2)×su(2)对称性的四能级激子二聚体上验证，将二阶和三阶输运路径算子与直接计算得到的精确投影输运矩阵对比。三阶展开能准确复现投影输运算子及其相关的路径混合效应。

Conclusion: SAKE不仅提供了一种高效的计算策略，还通过输运算子揭示了相干和耗散微扰如何在双面Feynman路径之间重新分配振幅，暴露了非线性光谱本身不易直接看出的机制信息，为非线性光谱学建立了支持高效局部参数探索、灵敏度分析和未来逆向设计应用的可微分计算框架。

Abstract: We introduce the Spectral Autodiff Kernel Expansion (SAKE), a differentiable computational framework for transporting nonlinear spectroscopic response between neighboring quantum dynamical models. Rather than recomputing multidimensional spectra independently for each Hamiltonian or Liouvillian, SAKE constructs local transport expansions about a reference model by combining forward-mode automatic differentiation with Duhamel transport theory. Automatic differentiation generates first-, second-, and third-order derivatives of the parameter-dependent Liouvillian, which are assembled into a pathway transport operator that maps the nonlinear response of a reference model onto neighboring systems. The framework is validated for a four-level excitonic dimer possessing an $su(2)\times su(2)$ symmetry by comparing second- and third-order transported pathway operators with exact projected transport matrices obtained from direct calculations. The third-order expansion accurately reproduces the projected transport operator and its associated pathway mixing. Beyond providing an efficient computational strategy, the transport operator reveals how coherent and dissipative perturbations redistribute amplitude among double-sided Feynman pathways, exposing mechanistic information that is not directly apparent from the nonlinear spectrum. SAKE thereby establishes a differentiable computational framework for nonlinear spectroscopy that supports efficient local parameter exploration, sensitivity analysis, and future inverse-design applications.

</details>


### [132] [Preparation of Large Fock States in Resonators with High Probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20133)
*Lucas R. S. Santos,Ciro M. Diniz,Daniel Z. Rossatto,Celso J. Villas-Boas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合量子非破坏性（QND）光子数编码与量子振幅放大（QAA）的协议，以实现高概率、宣布式（heralded）的大Fock态制备，在电路QED等玻色子平台上可用最多8个量子比特的寄存器近确定性制备具有数百个激发的Fock态，并可作为生成两模NOON态的扩展基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大Fock态是玻色子量子信息和量子增强计量学的重要资源，但高激发数下高概率制备仍然困难：确定性方法需要日益复杂的控制，而基于测量的方法通常伴随低宣布概率（heralding probability）。因此需要一种新方案兼顾高概率与可行性。

Method: 协议从腔模制备于相干态出发，利用量子相位估计（QPE）将光子数信息编码到多量子比特寄存器中，再通过量子振幅放大（QAA）在测量前提升目标输出结果的概率。该方案可直接实现于色散电路QED，也可类比适配于具有QND光子数读出能力的其他玻色子平台（如腔QED）。

Result: 使用最多8个量子比特的寄存器，可实现近确定性制备具有数百个激发的Fock态。该协议还可作为通过条件分束器操作生成两模NOON态的第一阶段。

Conclusion: 该协议在高概率宣布式大Fock态制备方面具有显著优势，兼具广泛的平台适用性，并为进一步生成NOON态等更复杂的非经典量子态提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large Fock states are important resources for bosonic quantum information and quantum-enhanced metrology, but preparing them with high probability at large excitation numbers remains challenging, as deterministic methods become increasingly control-intensive, while measurement-based approaches typically suffer from low heralding probabilities. Here we propose a protocol that combines quantum nondemolition photon-number encoding with quantum amplitude amplification to enable high-probability heralded generation of large Fock states. Starting from a cavity mode prepared in a coherent state, Quantum Phase Estimation encodes photon-number information into a multi-qubit register, while Quantum Amplitude Amplification boosts the probability of a desired target outcome before measurement. The scheme has an immediate implementation in dispersive circuit-QED, but can be analogously adapted to other bosonic platforms with QND photon-number readout, such as cavity-QED. With a register of up to eight qubits, near-deterministic preparation of Fock states with hundreds of excitations is possible. We also show that the protocol can serve as the first stage of an extension toward generating a two-mode NOON state via a conditional beam-splitter operation.

</details>


### [133] [Vacuum viscosity and relativistic inertia: Motion of a massive object with charged internal degrees of freedom interacting with a classical field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20140)
*Jen-Tsung Hsiang,Bei-Lok Hu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个运动的大质量物体（M）与自由无界经典场（φ）相互作用时，其内部带电自由度（χ）是否会产生粘性力（类似宇宙粒子产生和动态卡西米尔效应中的真空粘滞）。研究发现非相对论计算给出"是"的答案，而相对论协变计算给出"否"的答案，作者定位了非相对论框架的缺陷，并得到了在特殊相对论约束下对牛顿第一、第二定律的丰富修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望追踪真空粘滞效应的起源——该效应源于量子场涨落，能够使宇宙膨胀各向同性化或减缓运动镜子的速度。通过研究一个极简化的微物理模型（运动大质量物体+内部带电自由度+自由经典场的三方相互作用），探究粘性力是否普遍存在，进而揭示非相对论与相对论框架之间的深层矛盾。

Method: 采用光力学（optomechanics）中的微物理模型，构建不等三方（χ-φ-M）相互作用的框架。首先进行非相对论计算（适用于原子物理需求），再进行相对论协变计算，对比两种方法的结果，并识别非相对论框架的缺陷来源。

Result: 非相对论计算得到"存在粘性力"（是）的答案，而相对论协变计算得到"不存在粘性力"（否）的答案。作者定位了非相对论框架中缺陷所在的位置，指出在实施特殊相对论原则时，牛顿第一和第二定律需被丰富和扩展。

Conclusion: 这个潜在但真实的矛盾在技术上非平凡、在物理上具有启发性。解决该矛盾需要对牛顿运动定律进行扩充（在特殊相对论约束下），并提供正确获取非相对论结果的规则。这一发现加深了对真空粘滞效应起源的理解。

Abstract: Our present investigation into a rather rudimentary problem is motivated by two classes of problems studied since the 70's, cosmological particle creation and its more accessible analog, the dynamical Casimir effect on the one hand, and quantum friction a neutral atom moving along a dielectric surface would experience, on the other. The backreaction effects of produced particles being able to isotropize the expansion of the universe, or to slow down the moving mirror can be understood via the concept of vacuum viscosity arising from fluctuations of the quantum field. We want to track down the origin of this effect by asking the question whether a moving massive $M$ object with a charged internal degrees of freedom $χ$ interacting with a free unbounded classical field $φ$ at zero temperature would experience a viscous force, similar to the said precedents. Adopting a microphysics model for optomechanics which can treat the unequal tripartite $χ$-$φ$-$M$ interactions, we first perform a nonrelativistic calculation, which seems perfectly legitimate considering the needs of atomic physics, and found the answer to be yes, but a relativistic covariant calculation says no. We identify where the nonrelativistic framework is defective. The resolution of this latent yet real conflict is technically nontrivial but physically quite inspirational. It results in added enriched contents to Newton's first and second laws when the principles of special relativity are enforced, and rules to follow to get the correct nonrelativistic answer

</details>


### [134] [Proper Learning of Shallow All-to-All Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20162)
*Steven Kordonowy,Jacob Watkins*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究浅层量子电路学习的变体问题：在已知查询访问和门排列结构的前提下，学习电路中使用的具体门，生成操作等价的电路。基于最近学习Haar随机砖墙电路的工作，提出一个基于电路前端和后端迭代局部门反转的元算法框架，并将其应用于随机全连接二局域电路，通过光锥增长分析发现该系综在深度 d* ~ log₂n + log₂log₂n 处发生尖锐的可学习性相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路学习是量子信息领域的核心问题，对量子计算验证、量子密码学等有重要意义。近期量子密码方案的安全性依赖于电路学习的难度，因此理解何种电路在何种条件下可被高效学习至关重要。本文希望推广已有针对Haar随机砖墙电路的学习方法，探索更广泛的电路类别，并揭示随机全连接二局域电路的可学习性边界。

Method: 提出一个元算法框架，核心思想是在电路前端和后端进行迭代的局部门反转（iterative local gate inversions），通过逐步"剥除"已知作用于边界量子比特的门来反向恢复电路中的未知门。将该框架应用于随机全连接二局域电路，并通过光锥增长（lightcone growth）分析来刻画电路的可学习性边界，结合解析和数值手段验证。

Result: 解析和数值证据表明，随机全连接二局域电路在大尺寸极限下存在一个尖锐的可学习性相变，临界深度约为 d* ~ log₂n + log₂log₂n。在该深度以下电路可以被有效学习，在该深度以上则不可学习。

Conclusion: 该结果为量子电路学习的可学习性边界提供了新的理论刻画，对基于电路学习难度构建的量子密码方案具有重要启示。同时指出该结果与现有密码方案设定之间存在重要区别，为未来研究提供了方向。

Abstract: This work considers a variation on the problem of learning shallow quantum circuits. Given query access to the circuit, as well as knowledge of its gate layout, we consider the task of learning the specific gates used in the circuit, producing an operationally-equivalent circuit matching this structure. Building on recent work for learning Haar random brickwork circuits, we identify a meta-algorithmic framework for learning broad classes of circuits based on iterative local gate inversions at the front and back of the circuit. We apply these techniques to study random, all-to-all, two-local circuits, and provide analytical and numerical evidence that this ensemble undergoes a sharp learnability transition at depth $d^* \sim \log_2 n + \log_2\log_2 n$ in the large size limit, based on an analysis of lightcone growth. These results have implications for recently proposed quantum cryptographic schemes based on the difficulty of circuit learning, though there are important distinctions with respect to our setting that suggest avenues for future study.

</details>


### [135] [Architecture and Compilation Co-Design for High-Rate Quantum Product Codes on Neutral Atom Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20164)
*Adrian Liu,Wan-Hsuan Lin,Daniel Bochen Tan,Qian Xu,Jason Cong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出ONEX框架，将量子LDPC码的二维物理执行规划分解为独立的一维子问题，利用SMT编码求得深度最优解，并通过多阶段编译流水线（包括任意时间优化、移动压缩和迭代反馈）实现高效编译。在超图乘积码映射到中性原子阵列的场景下，相比构造性1D算法和通用2D编译器，时钟速率分别提升3.7x-6.1x和29.8x-42.1x，可扩展至2500个数据量子比特。该框架还扩展到分区布局和提升乘积码家族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模容错量子计算需要高编码率的量子纠错码，qLDPC码是重要候选，尤其结合中性原子阵列的动态长程连接。然而，为QEC合成有效且高效的物理执行计划被证明是困难的组合优化问题，形成关键编译瓶颈，且随着码尺寸增大而恶化。当前的通用2D编译器难以应对这一复杂度，亟需针对特定码族的高效解决方案。

Method: ONEX框架采用"降维"策略：将复杂的2D物理执行规划分解为独立的一维子问题。具体包括：(1) 用SMT编码显式构造一维执行计划，证明可求得深度最优解；(2) 构建多阶段编译流水线，融合任意时间优化、运动压缩和迭代反馈机制，在保持实用编译时间的同时提供渐进优化和按需检索高质量解；(3) 将框架部署到超图乘积码内存映射到中性原子阵列的编译场景，并扩展至分区布局和提升乘积码家族。

Result: 在超图乘积码映射到中性原子阵列的实验中，ONEX相比构造性1D算法实现3.7x-6.1x的时钟速率提升，相比通用2D编译器实现29.8x-42.1x的时钟速率提升；可高效扩展到2500个数据量子比特的码规模。此外，扩展到分区布局后揭示了相应的架构权衡，并通过代表性实例展示了其对更广泛的提升乘积码家族的适用性。

Conclusion: ONEX通过将高维编译问题分解为可独立求解的一维子问题，在保证执行深度最优性的同时实现了显著的编译效率提升，验证了以特定码族为目标的设计思路在量子编译中优于通用方法的有效性。该框架在分区布局和提升乘积码上的扩展表明其具备良好的通用性和实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Achieving fault-tolerant quantum computing at a practical scale demands quantum error correction (QEC) codes with high encoding rates. Quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes emerge as a promising candidate, especially given the rise of neutral atom arrays that provide dynamic long-range connectivity via atom movements. In general, synthesizing valid and efficient physical execution plans for QEC is a provably hard combinatorial problem, forming a critical compilation bottleneck that worsens as code sizes grow. To overcome this complexity, we focus on an important product family of qLDPC codes with dimension-reduction properties, and propose ONEX. This framework decomposes complex 2D physical execution planning into independent 1D subproblems, each solved to optimal execution depth within practical compilation time. First, we formulate the 1D execution plan with an explicit satisfiability modulo theories (SMT) encoding. This protocol produces provably depth-optimal solutions with substantial duration reduction. Second, we develop a multi-stage compilation pipeline featuring anytime optimization, movement compaction, and iterative feedback. This pipeline maintains practical wall-clock times while providing progressive refinement and on-demand retrieval of quality solutions. Third, we evaluate ONEX in the application of hypergraph product (HGP) code memory mapped onto neutral atom arrays, achieving 3.7x to 6.1x and 29.8x to 42.1x higher clock rates than the constructive 1D algorithm and the general 2D compiler, respectively, while scaling efficiently to codes with 2,500 data qubits. Finally, we extend ONEX to zoned layouts, revealing architectural insights into the associated trade-offs, and demonstrate its applicability to the broader lifted-product (LP) code family through a representative example.

</details>


### [136] [Large Scale Entanglement Structure Detection in 100-Qubit Systems via Local Joint Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20170)
*Rui Li,Yuhang Wang,Chunxiao Du,Shikun Zhang,Zheng Qin,Wenxiu Li,Hao Zhang,Zhisong Xiao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的框架，仅通过局部相关指纹即可直接识别大规模量子态的分块纠缠结构。通过选择满足边界匹配条件p_1=p_R的局部Pauli基，可在单一测量配置下读出整个链，测量成本与系统大小无关。在噪声模拟中，该方法对多达100个量子比特系统中30种候选分区（GHZ、W、簇型）的平均分类准确率超过95%；在超导量子处理器上，对最多13个量子比特的分块结构进行了可靠验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别多体量子态的纠缠结构（即其组成部分如何划分为非纠缠区块）是量子信息科学的核心任务，但传统层析方法随系统规模呈指数级扩展，无法适用于近期的中等规模量子器件。

Method: 提出一个可扩展框架，直接从局部相关指纹识别大规模纠缠结构。关键思想是选择满足边界匹配条件p_1=p_R的局部Pauli基，使整个量子链可在单一测量配置下一次性读出，测量工作量与系统大小无关。

Result: 在噪声模拟中，该单基底协议在最多100个量子比特的系统中，对30种候选分区的GHZ、W和簇型结构平均分类准确率超过95%。在超导量子处理器上，对最多13个量子比特的系统可靠分类分块结构，更大规模时因噪声和深度引起的退化而失效。

Conclusion: 通过明确映射这些失效模式，本文界定了硬件级可扩展性的边界，并为在近期量子设备上表征纠缠结构提供了具体策略。

Abstract: Identifying the entanglement structure of a many-body quantum state, namely how its constituents partition into unentangled blocks, is a central task in quantum information science, yet conventional tomography scales exponentially with system size. Here we introduce a scalable framework that recognizes large-scale entanglement structures directly from local correlation fingerprints. By choosing a representative local Pauli basis that satisfies a boundary-matching condition p_1 = p_R, the entire chain is read out in a single measurement configuration, keeping the measurement effort independent of system size. In noisy simulations, this single-basis protocol classifies GHZ-, W-, and cluster-type structures among 30 candidate partitions with a mean accuracy exceeding 95% for systems of up to 100 qubits. We further validate the protocol on a superconducting quantum processor, where it reliably classifies block structures for systems of up to 13 qubits before noise- and depth-induced degradation sets in at larger sizes. By mapping these failure modes explicitly, our results delineate the boundary of hardware-level scalability and point to a concrete strategy for characterizing entanglement structure on near-term quantum devices.

</details>


### [137] [Characterization of a damping channel as a mixture of amplitude damping and anti-damping channels of different parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20178)
*Vijay Pathak,R. Srikanth*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个构建更广泛相位协变量子动力学的一般框架，通过以不等衰减参数和随时间变化的混合概率混合振幅阻尼与反振幅阻尼通道，实现了对收缩和翻译的独立控制，并能在非单性与单性区域之间连续调谐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非单性噪声是开放量子系统的基本特征，涉及能量交换和Bloch球上的态平移。虽然这些平移在态制备和信道容量中有用，但量子信息处理（特别是纠错）往往更偏好不发生平移的单性动力学。现有的相位协变动力学模型（如广义振幅阻尼信道）对这些特征的控制能力有限，缺乏灵活的参数调控手段。

Method: 构造性地将振幅阻尼和反振幅阻尼通道以不等衰减参数和依赖时间的混合概率进行混合，生成更广泛的相位协变动力学。采用一般的P可分性定理，通过在适当选择的Hilbert空间基下评估该定理，得到单性和非单性情形下简化的可分性条件。

Result: 该方法能够独立控制收缩与平移，允许在非单性和单性区域间连续调谐，并产生GAD中不存在的有效退相干贡献。框架涵盖了CP可分、P可分但非CP可分、以及非P可分等多种动力学行为。此外，适当的混合可以减小与理想无噪声演化的偏差，且即使在调谐到单性区域后这种改善仍然保持。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种超越标准热模型的开放量子系统动力学工程化方法，为噪声控制和量子信息处理提供了潜在应用。

Abstract: Non-unital noise is a fundamental feature of open quantum systems, governing energy exchange and inducing state translations on the Bloch sphere. While such translations can be useful for tasks such as state preparation and channel capacity, unital dynamics where no translation occurs are often preferred in quantum information processing, particularly for error correction. Phase-covariant dynamics provide a general framework encompassing dissipation, excitation, and dephasing processes in qubit systems; however, commonly used models such as the generalized amplitude damping (GAD) channel offer only limited control over these features. In this work, we present a constructive framework for generating a broader class of phase-covariant dynamics by mixing amplitude-damping and anti-damping channels with unequal decay parameters and time-dependent mixing probabilities. This approach enables independent control over contraction and translation, allows continuous tuning between non-unital and unital regimes, and yields an effective dephasing contribution absent in GAD. To characterize these dynamics, we employ the general theorem for P-divisibility and show that it can be used constructively by evaluating it in suitably chosen Hilbert space bases, leading to simplified conditions for both unital and non-unital cases. The framework captures a wide range of behaviors, including CP-divisible, P-divisible but not CP-divisible, and non-P-divisible dynamics. We further show that appropriate mixing can reduce the deviation from the ideal noiseless evolution and that such improvement persists even after tuning the dynamics to the unital regime. Our results provide a flexible approach to engineering open system dynamics beyond standard thermal models, with potential applications in noise control and quantum information processing.

</details>


### [138] [Hybrid Qubit-Rotor Quantum Systems: Clifford Structure, Universal Control, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20227)
*Dengyao Luo,Arvin Kushwaha,Mastawal Tirfe,Bojko N. Bakalov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了由n个量子比特和r个U(1)量子转子组成的混合量子寄存器，建立了其Clifford理论并证明了通用控制结果。作者对混合相空间的保Weyl对易关系自同构进行了完全分类，为每个自同构给出了显式的有限Clifford电路，并证明添加转子余弦势和一个固定的量子比特-转子条件相位后可在强算子拓扑下实现通用控制。该理论还被应用于精确受控位移实现规范协变物质跳跃、转子相位估计以及转子动量编码上的有限傅里叶变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: U(1)量子转子在分子旋转、超导相位-电荷电路和紧致规范场中普遍出现。将这种转子与量子比特相干耦合形成混合寄存器后，其控制结构既不同于振子-量子比特系统也不同于量子比特系统，需要建立独立的理论框架来理解其Clifford结构、通用控制性质以及实际应用潜力。

Method: 论文通过以下方法展开：(1)对混合相空间F_2^{2n}×Z^r×T^r上所有保持Weyl对易关系的自同构进行分类，并为每个自同构构造显式有限Clifford电路；(2)提供混合量子比特-转子耦合的规范形式，并确定合成它们所需的基本混合门的最小数目；(3)在局部Clifford操作基础上加入转子余弦势和一个固定的量子比特-转子条件相位，证明在强算子拓扑下实现通用控制；(4)将该框架应用于三个具体场景：规范协变物质跳跃的精确受控位移实现、转子相位估计与探针优化、转子动量编码上的有限傅里叶变换及其编译。

Result: 分类是方向性的：转子动量奇偶校验可以控制Clifford群内的量子比特Pauli操作，而每个非零的量子比特控制转子动量位移都是非Clifford的。在通用控制方面，局部Clifford操作加上转子余弦势和一个固定条件相位即可在强算子拓扑下实现通用控制。具体应用中，d=2^s的单转子傅里叶变换可编译为O(s)个动量选择和受控位移指令，每个跨寄存器傅里叶因子由一个二次转子Clifford门实现。

Conclusion: 本文建立了量子比特-转子混合寄存器的Clifford理论框架，揭示了此类系统独特的方向性控制结构（转子到比特的奇偶宇称控制在Clifford内，而比特到转子的位移控制必然非Clifford），并展示了该理论在规范场模拟、相位估计和傅里叶变换编译等量子信息处理任务中的实用价值。

Abstract: A $U(1)$ quantum rotor pairs a periodic angle with an integer-valued conjugate momentum, and occurs in molecular rotation, superconducting phase-charge circuits, and compact gauge fields. Coupling such a rotor coherently to qubits gives a hybrid register whose control structure is not inherited from either the oscillator-qubit or the qudit case. We develop a Clifford theory, together with a universal-control result, for registers of $n$ qubits and $r$ rotors. We classify all automorphisms of the hybrid phase space $\mathbb{F}_2^{2n}\times\mathbb{Z}^r\times\mathbb{T}^r$ that preserve the Weyl commutation relations, and give an explicit finite Clifford circuit for each one. The classification is directional: rotor momentum parity may control qubit Pauli operations within the Clifford group, while every nonzero qubit-controlled rotor momentum shift is non-Clifford. It also yields normal forms for the mixed qubit-rotor couplings and the exact minimum number of elementary mixed gates needed to synthesize them. Adding a rotor cosine potential and one fixed qubit-rotor conditional phase to the local Clifford operations gives universal control on the full Hilbert space in the strong operator topology. We then apply this structure in three settings: an exact controlled-shift realization of gauge-covariant matter hopping, which is necessarily non-Clifford; rotor phase estimation with direct angle readout and probe optimization under momentum-support and energy constraints; and finite Fourier transforms on rotor momentum codes, where the one-rotor transform for $d=2^s$ compiles into $O(s)$ momentum-selective and controlled-shift instructions and each cross-register Fourier factor is implemented by one quadratic rotor Clifford gate.

</details>


### [139] [Jamming Extensions of Quantum Correlations Lead to Hidden Superluminal Signaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20247)
*Ravishankar Ramanathan,Xie Sicheng,Michał Eckstein,Paweł Horodecki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了与相对论因果性(RC)兼容的关联集合，证明了在量子态约束下，任何非平凡jamming效应（无论与态无关还是与态相关）必然导致隐藏的超光速信号传递，并由此构造了能破解设备无关(DI)比特承诺和秘密共享协议的RC攻击，表明针对相对论性对手的安全性需要将行为与测量事件的时空位置共同指定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论因果性在特定时空配置下允许jamming现象——一种不产生可探知超光速信号但确实存在的超光速因果影响。此前的研究没有完整刻画RC兼容的关联集合，也不清楚在量子态约束下jamming是否仍然一致地成立，更不清楚这种RC约束对设备无关密码学原语的安全性有何影响。

Method: 通过扩展PRL 104, 140404中的算子框架，完整刻画相对论因果性兼容的关联集合。在量子态约束下，分别考察状态无关和状态相关的jamming情形，证明任何非平凡jamming必然导致隐藏的超光速信号传递。以此表征为基础，在jamming几何中构造针对DI比特承诺和DI秘密共享的显式RC攻击。

Result: 完整刻画了相对论因果性兼容的关联集合；证明了在量子态约束下，任何非平凡jamming（无论态无关或态相关）必然导致隐藏的超光速信号传递，因此仅有的两种一致可能性是：无jamming的标准量子关联，或完全的相对论因果关联集合。此外，构造了能破解在no-signaling对手下仍安全的DI比特承诺和DI秘密共享协议的显式RC攻击。

Conclusion: 针对仅受相对论因果性约束的对手，安全性的建立要求行为的指定必须附带测量事件的时空位置信息，仅凭输入输出统计量不足以保证安全。这为DI密码学协议在相对论性对手模型下的安全性分析提供了新的判定标准。

Abstract: Relativistic causality in certain spacetime configurations permits jamming - superluminal causal influences that nevertheless do not enable superluminal signaling. We fully characterize the correlations compatible with relativistic causality (RC) by extending the operator framework of PRL 104, 140404. When the underlying state is required to be quantum, we prove that any nontrivial jamming - whether state-independent or state-dependent - necessarily leads to hidden superluminal signaling. Thus, the only consistent possibilities are standard quantum correlations without jamming or the full relativistically causal correlation set, with intermediate jamming extensions of quantum correlations leading to signaling. As an application of our characterization, we investigate the security of device-independent (DI) cryptographic primitives against adversaries constrained only by relativistic causality. We construct explicit RC attacks that break DI bit commitment and DI secret sharing in jamming geometries, even when these protocols remain secure against no-signaling adversaries. Our results show that security against relativistic adversaries requires behaviors to be specified together with the spacetime locations of their measurement events; input-output statistics alone are insufficient.

</details>


### [140] [Logarithmic depth compression of Heisenberg Hamiltonian simulation by fan-out parallelization, with built-in error detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20250)
*Artemiy Burov,Clément Javerzac*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于fan-out的gadget编译器，通过将每个逻辑自旋编码为按交互度数确定大小的重复码寄存器，将Heisenberg型NMR哈密顿量的窄深电路转换为浅而宽的电路，并以对数深度CNOT fan-out并行执行所有给定Pauli类型的成对交互。在heavy-hex超导和全连接离子阱两种目标平台上，对包含高连通度中心的交互图，体积最优调度可将二比特深度减半，13自旋演示系统体积降低1.7倍；但增益随交互图度分布的不均匀性增大而增加，对密集均匀图则退化为顺序电路最优。在基于现代处理器校准的噪声模型下，gadget电路需依赖内置错误检测的后选择，且误差率再降低1到1.5个数量级才能匹配或超越顺序编译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中等规模含噪量子计算机受限于电路深度，而自旋系统的乘积公式模拟会产生窄而深的电路，这限制了在深度受限硬件上的可执行规模。作者希望探索用宽度换取深度的编译策略，并在真实硬件约束下进行公平的资源比较。

Method: 将每个逻辑自旋编码为大小由其交互度数决定的小型重复码寄存器；所有给定Pauli类型的成对交互通过对数深度CNOT fan-out并行执行；冗余寄存器提供后选择的错误检测且不增加额外算法开销。针对heavy-hex超导和全连接离子阱两种目标进行transpile，对一组NMR自旋系统进行固定协议的资源比较。

Result: 对含高连通度中心的交互图，体积最优调度使二比特深度减半，13自旋演示系统体积降低1.7倍（heavy-hex上还降低了二比特门数）；在全连接平台上最高度分子深度降低2.5倍，但所有系统的二比特门数均上升。增益随交互图度不均匀性增大而增大，对密集均匀图退化为顺序电路最优。在缩放自现代处理器校准的噪声模型下，gadget电路需后选择且误差率改善1-1.5个数量级才能匹配或超越顺序编译。

Conclusion: fan-out gadget编译策略在深度受限硬件上对具有度不均匀交互图的系统具有明确的体积优势，但其实际效用高度依赖噪声水平和可用的误差检测后选择机制。当前噪声水平下优势有限，但随着误差率改进约一个数量级，gadget方案将在实际硬件上展现出竞争力。

Abstract: Noisy intermediate-scale quantum computers are constrained by circuit depth, while product-formula simulation of spin systems leads to narrow and deep circuits. Here we introduce a fan-out-based gadget compiler that trades circuit depth for width in simulations of Heisenberg-type nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) Hamiltonians. Each logical spin is encoded into a small repetition-code register sized by its interaction degree, so that all pairwise interactions of a given Pauli type execute in parallel after a logarithmic-depth CNOT fan-out, and the redundant registers provide error detection for post-selection at no additional algorithmic overhead. The central result is a fixed-protocol resource comparison of the two compilations, transpiled to heavy-hex superconducting and all-to-all trapped-ion targets across a set of NMR spin systems. For interaction graphs with a high-degree hub the volume-optimal schedule halves the two-qubit depth and reduces the volume 1.7-fold for the 13-spin demonstration, which on heavy-hex also lowers the two-qubit gate count, and the depth reduction rises to 2.5-fold on all-to-all for the highest-degree molecule studied. On all-to-all the two-qubit gate count rises for every system, so the volume reduction is a benefit on depth-limited hardware. The gain grows with the degree inhomogeneity of the interaction graph and vanishes for dense uniform graphs, where the optimum is the sequential circuit. We simulate the zero-field NMR spectrum of tetramethylsilane, a 13-spin star system. Under a noise model scaled from a published present-day processor calibration, the shallower gadget circuits match or surpass the sequential compilation only after post-selection on their built-in error detection, once error rates improve by one to one and a half orders of magnitude. We verify the spectra against an independent classical computation.

</details>


### [141] [Parallel Quantum Advantage with Limited Adaptivity Requires Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20297)
*Qipeng Liu,Saachi Mutreja*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子查询计算领域取得了重要进展：证明了Aaronson和Ambainis关于量子查询算法几乎处处经典模拟的猜想对于大规模并行量子查询算法成立。作者通过证明并行量子查询算法无法区分均匀分布预言机与"稠密分布"预言机，建立了耦合定理，并将这一方法推广到具有有界量子查询前缀、混合自适经典查询以及常数轮自适应性的量子算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Aaronson-Ambainis猜想：量子查询算法是否在几乎所有输入上可以被经典算法高效模拟。该猜想的高层直觉是量子指数加速只能在结构化的输入上实现。此前的相关工作（Yamakawa和Zhandry）表明并行量子查询在抽样问题上仍可实现指数加速，因此该猜想在并行设置下并不显然成立，需要专门的技术突破。

Method: 作者的核心技术贡献是建立了一个耦合定理，将均匀分布预言机与稠密分布预言机关联起来，从而证明并行量子查询算法无法区分这两类分布。基于这一基础，进一步将该方法推广至三种更一般的场景：(1) 有界量子查询前缀后接大规模并行量子查询；(2) 在并行量子查询前进行任意多项式次自适应经典查询的混合算法；(3) 以并行查询模拟定理为基例，推导常数轮自适应性量子算法的模拟定理。

Result: 证明了对大规模并行量子查询算法，Aaronson-Ambainis猜想成立，即存在经典模拟方案能在(1-δ)的输入上以ε加性误差近似其接受概率，仅需poly(T, 1/ε, 1/δ)次经典查询。同时将结果扩展至具有有界量子前缀的算法、混合经典-量子算法以及常数轮自适应量子算法。

Conclusion: 该工作显著推进了Aaronson-Ambainis猜想的研究，为量子查询算法的经典模拟提供了更广泛的适用场景。尽管并行量子查询在抽样问题上可超越经典算法，但在接受概率的几乎处处模拟上经典算法仍然高效，这加深了对量子加速所需结构条件的理解。

Abstract: Aaronson and Ambainis (Theory of Computing, 2014) conjectured that quantum query algorithms admit efficient almost-everywhere classical simulation: for any $T$-query quantum algorithm, its acceptance probability can be approximated on a $(1-δ)$ fraction of inputs, up to $ε$ additive error, using $\mathrm{poly}(T, 1/ε, 1/δ)$ classical queries. At a high level, the conjecture suggests that exponential quantum speedups are possible only on sufficiently structured inputs.
  In this work, we make progress on this conjecture by proving it for quantum algorithms that make massively parallel quantum queries. In contrast, Yamakawa and Zhandry (Journal of the ACM, 2024) showed that quantum algorithms restricted to parallel queries can still achieve exponential speedups over classical algorithms for sampling problems. We establish our simulation theorem by proving the stronger statement that parallel-query quantum algorithms cannot distinguish the uniform distribution over oracles from oracles drawn from so-called "dense distributions". Our main technical contribution is a coupling theorem that relates the uniform distribution over oracles to oracles drawn from dense distributions.
  We further extend this approach beyond the purely parallel setting, obtaining simulation theorems both for algorithms with a bounded quantum-query prefix followed by a massively parallel quantum-query stage, and for hybrid algorithms that make an arbitrary polynomial number of adaptive classical queries before the massively parallel quantum-query stage. Finally, using the parallel-query simulation theorem as a base case, we obtain simulation theorems for quantum algorithms with constant rounds of adaptivity.

</details>


### [142] [Group-theoretic treatment of strong light-matter coupling with an arbitrary number of excitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20340)
*Antti Peltola,Olli Siltanen,Kimmo Luoma,Konstantinos S. Daskalakis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用量子发射体的组合结构，系统地研究了具有任意激发数的Tavis-Cummings模型，推导了其本征系统的结构特性，识别了实际规模系统中的辐射跃迁，并提供了一个降低此类指数增长希尔伯特空间系统计算复杂度的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学微腔中强光-物质相互作用产生极化激元（polaritons），已在现代技术中被广泛应用。然而，现有理论描述通常局限于单激发情形，无法捕捉多激发物理现象，阻碍了进一步的技术进步。

Method: 利用量子发射体的组合（combinatorial）结构来研究任意激发数的Tavis-Cummings模型，推导其本征系统的结构与性质，并据此识别实际规模系统中允许的辐射跃迁。

Result: 揭示了无法在少激发区间内观测到的新行为，同时提供了一个降低具有指数增长希尔伯特空间的类似系统计算复杂度的框架。

Conclusion: 该工作将Tavis-Cummings模型的理论从少激发扩展至任意激发数，发现了新的物理行为，并提出了简化此类多体量子系统计算的方法，对极化激元物理及腔量子电动力学领域具有重要价值。

Abstract: Strong light-matter interactions in optical microcavities give rise to hybrid light-matter states known as polaritons. While actively used in modern technologies, theoretical descriptions of such systems are often restricted to the single-excitation case, limiting their ability to capture many-excitation physics and hindering further technological advancements. Here, by exploiting the combinatorial structure of quantum emitters, we investigate the Tavis-Cummings model with arbitrary number of excitations. We derive the structure and properties of its eigensystem and identify allowed radiative transitions in systems of realistic size scales. Our work reveals new behavior inaccessible to the few-excitation regime, while also providing a framework to reduce the computational complexity of similar systems with exponentially growing Hilbert spaces.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [A minimality criterion for surfaces of general type with applications to product-quotient surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19461)
*Federico Fallucca*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个数值判据来建立一般型曲面的极小性，并将其应用于乘积-商曲面情形，用于判定(-1)-曲线是否被约束在两条自然纤维化之一的纤维内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在代数几何中，确定一个一般型曲面是否为极小曲面（即不包含(-1)-曲线）是分类理论中的基本问题。特别地，对于乘积-商曲面这类构造，需要有效的数值方法来判定极小性以及(-1)-曲线的位置。

Method: 提出一个数值判据来检验一般型曲面的极小性；随后将该判据专门化到乘积-商曲面（product-quotient surface）的情形，在此类曲面具有两条自然纤维化的结构下，判据进一步确定(-1)-曲线是否必须位于其中某条纤维内部。

Result: 得到了一个判定一般型曲面极小性的数值准则，并成功将其推广到乘积-商曲面情形，能够确定(-1)-曲线在这类曲面上的位置约束（即是否完全落在某条自然纤维的纤维内）。

Conclusion: 该工作为一般型曲面的极小性判定提供了数值工具，尤其对乘积-商曲面给出了更精细的几何信息，即(-1)-曲线与两条自然纤维化的位置关系。

Abstract: We present a numerical criterion to establish the minimality of surfaces of general type. This approach is then adapted to the case of product-quotient surfaces, where the criterion determines whether the (-1)-curves are constrained to lie inside a fibre of one of the two natural fibrations.

</details>


### [144] [Geometrically multiplicative non-archimedean norms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19481)
*Michael Temkin,Grigory Zutler*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了实值域上Banach代数范数的乘法性、谱性与谱乘法性在基域扩张下的保持问题，得到了与经典几何不可约性和可约性理论相类似的确切判据，并在若干方面推广了Poineau的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Berkovich在非阿基米德几何中使用了万能（几何）乘法范数，Poineau对此做了详细研究。但关于范数的乘法性、谱性及谱乘法性在基域扩张下何时被保持这一问题，尚未有系统的研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过对完备非阿基米德域上Banach代数范数的性质在基域扩张（实值域扩张）下的行为进行深入分析，建立与经典几何不可约性和可约性理论类比的确切判据。

Result: 得到了范数乘法性、谱性和谱乘法性在基域扩张下保持的精确判据，并在若干方面推广了Poineau的已有结果。

Conclusion: 本文为实值域上Banach代数范数在基域扩张下的性质保持问题提供了系统性的理论框架和精确判据，进一步丰富了非阿基米德几何中范数理论的内容。

Abstract: Universally (or geometrically) multiplicative norms on Banach algebras over complete non-archimedean fields were used by Berkovich in his works on non-archimedean geometry, and later they were studied in some detail by Poineau. In this paper, we perform a more thorough study of the question when multiplicativity, spectrality and spectral multiplicativity of norms on algebras over real valued fields are preserved by ground field extensions. We obtain precise criteria quite analogous to the classical theory of geometric irreducibility and reducedness. In particular, we generalize the results of Poineau in a few aspects.

</details>


### [145] [Quadratic generation of ideals defining nonsigular toric 3-folds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19577)
*Shoetsu Ogata*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类特殊的射影线丛（整体二上）在充分大射影空间中的嵌入是射影正规的，且其理想由二次元素生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究射影簇的射影正规性与二次生成理想（即满足"二次性"性质）是代数几何中的基本问题，对射影线丛这类结构，确定其嵌入的理想生成次数具有理论意义。

Method: 研究对象为射影平面沿至多4个不变点的爆破，或两条射影直线乘积沿至多4个不变点的爆破所得到的非奇异环面曲面上的射影线丛X。对X上的任意丰沛线丛L，研究其定义的大射影空间嵌入的射影正规性及理想生成元次数。

Result: 证明了该嵌入是射影正规的，并且嵌入后的理想由二次元素生成。

Conclusion: 对于上述特定类型的环面曲面上的射影线丛，其丰沛线丛定义的嵌入满足射影正规性且理想的生成元次数不高于2。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a projective line bundle over a nonsingular toric surface which is a blowup the projective plane along at most 4 invariant points, or a blowup the product of the projective lines along at most 4 invariant points. Let $L$ be an ample line bundle on $X$. Then $L$ defines a projectively normal embedding to big projective space. We show the ideal of this embedded $X$ is generated by elements of degree two.

</details>


### [146] [Explicit birational models of marked elliptic surfaces of relative degree two](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19970)
*Sönke Rollenske,Anna Ulivi,Aline Zanardini*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了带有双截面（或更一般地，度为2的相对极化）及双纤维的椭圆曲面的两种不同双有理模型的显式构造方法，并展示了如何在toric簇中简洁描述指标为2的Halphen曲面和Enriques曲面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆曲面带有双截面和双纤维的几何结构具有特殊的重要性，但缺乏显式的构造方法。通过显式构造双有理模型，可以为Halphen曲面和Enriques曲面提供更简单、更直观的描述方式。

Method: 作者提出了两种不同的双有理模型构造方法，针对带有双截面（或度为2的相对极化）和双纤维的椭圆曲面。随后将这些构造方法应用于指标为2的Halphen曲面和Enriques曲面，展示了它们在toric簇中的简单描述。

Result: 成功建立了两种显式构造方案，并在toric簇的框架下给出了指标2的Halphen曲面和Enriques曲面的简洁几何描述。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种新的视角来理解和构造带有双截面与双纤维的椭圆曲面，为Halphen曲面和Enriques曲面的研究提供了更具体的几何工具。

Abstract: We introduce explicit construction methods for two distinct birational models of elliptic surfaces equipped with a bisection --- or, more generally, a relative polarisation of degree two --- and double fibres. For Halphen surfaces of index two and Enriques surfaces, we illustrate how these give simple descriptions inside a toric variety.

</details>


### [147] [Universal torsors over quartic del Pezzo surfaces and stable rationality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20029)
*Yuri Tschinkel,Zhijia Zhang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明：在特征为零的域k上，光滑四次del Pezzo曲面S上的通用环空间（universal torsor）T若具有k-点，则T是k-有理的。作为应用，获得了在每个维数大于2的Q上光滑三次超曲面的稳定有理（stably rational）例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究del Pezzo曲面上的通用环空间的k-有理性问题，这是算术代数几何中关于有理性（rationality）和稳定有理性（stable rationality）的核心问题之一，并为立方超曲面的稳定有理性质提供新的构造方法。

Method: 作者通过分析光滑四次del Pezzo曲面上的通用环空间的结构，利用其几何性质（如S中包含足够的例外曲线）来证明在存在k-点的条件下T是k-有理的；随后将这一结果应用于构造有理点上的稳定有理三次超曲面。

Result: 证明了四次del Pezzo曲面上的通用环空间在具有k-点时是k-有理的；并由此在所有维数大于2的Q上构造出稳定有理的光滑三次超曲面的例子。

Conclusion: 该结果推广了关于通用环空间有理性的已知结论，并通过四次del Pezzo曲面的通用环空间给出了高维稳定有理三次超曲面的新例子，丰富了稳定有理流形的研究。

Abstract: Let $S$ be a smooth quartic del Pezzo surface over a field $k$, of characteristic zero. We prove that a universal torsor $\mathcal T$ over $S$ is $k$-rational, provided $\mathcal T$ has $k$-points. As an application, we obtain examples of stably rational smooth cubic hypersurfaces over $\mathbb Q$ in every dimension greater than 2.

</details>


### [148] [Resolution of Singularities in Positive Characteristic: Frobenius-Hasse Towers and Exceptional-History Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20066)
*Chenxiao Tian*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在正特征p>0的完美域k上，提出了规范强嵌入消解与主理想化的对象级构造程序。该程序以"带地址的比较因子良基历史"取代逐点数值不变量的单调性，通过标记Rees代数的微分-积分饱和，构造了全Hasse活性包、过滤系数立方体、半线性Frobenius-Hasse源及字面变换数据，并借助六行缺陷演算将局部问题路由至曲面、环面单项式、二项式及加性型过程。核心结构为全局替换证书，证明其可支持单一依赖良基序下的严格多重集替换，从而保证迭代终止并重构出与有序边界正规相交的正则严格变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正特征代数几何中，经典的特征零消解方法依赖数值不变量（如Hironaka的H-函数）的单调下降，但在正特征下这些不变量往往不具单调性（例如Higman类型的例子）。本文旨在构建一个不依赖数值不变量单调性的对象级（object-level）构造程序，为规范强嵌入消解与主理想化提供统一且可执行的证明框架，同时需处理完全wild分支（不可分扩展）带来的技术困难。

Method: 方法包含以下核心构件：(1) 标记Rees代数的微分-积分饱和构造全Hasse活性包；(2) 过滤系数立方体与半线性Frobenius-Hasse源提取字面变换数据；(3) 六行缺陷演算将问题分类路由至曲面、环面单项式、二项式和加性型过程；(4) 清洁中心组合序列化并在全局神经上下降；(5) 核心的全局替换证书，携带后继地址、有偿商、类型化迹、显示父代、重开数据及终结真理，通过刚性生成、跨代不重置、完全wild容量控制、中心或类型化出口选择、结构化余纤维及显示父代分配实现对象级实现，最终在单一依赖良基序中证明严格多重集替换。

Result: 证明了配备全局替换证书的完整状态在单一依赖良基序中允许严格多重集替换，从而保证算法迭代终止；终止后的穷尽状态可重构出与有序边界正规相交的正则严格变换。同时给出了证书的对象级实现方案，涵盖刚性生成、跨代无重置、完全wild容量控制等关键机制。

Conclusion: 本文建立了正特征下规范强嵌入消解与主理想化的对象级构造理论，通过良基历史与替换证书替代传统数值不变量的单调性论证，为正特征消解问题提供了完整且可机械执行的证明路径，填补了该领域在完全wild情形下的空白。

Abstract: Let k be a perfect field of characteristic p>0. We introduce an object-level construction for canonical strong embedded resolution and principalization over k. The construction program replaces monotonicity of pointwise numerical invariants by a well-founded history of addressed comparison factors. Starting from the differential-integral saturation of a marked Rees algebra, we construct total-Hasse activity packets, filtered coefficient cubes, semilinear Frobenius-Hasse sources, and literal transform data for ordinary permissible blowups. A six-row defect calculus routes local problems to certified surface, toroidal-monomial, binomial, and additive-type procedures, after which clean centre portfolios are serialized and descended on a global nerve.
  The central structure is a global replacement certificate transporting successor addresses, paid quotients, typed traces, displayed parents, reopening data, and terminal truth across macroblocks. We prove that a complete state equipped with this certificate admits a strict multiset replacement in a single dependent well-founded order; hence the iteration terminates and the exhausted state reconstructs a regular strict transform having normal crossings with the ordered boundary. We further formulate an object-level realization of the certificate through rigid generation, cross-generation no-reset, complete wild-capacity control, a centre-or-typed-exit alternative, structured cofibres, displayed-parent allocation, and literal terminal truth. The program page is also available at website https://sites.google.com/view/positive-char-resolution

</details>


### [149] [The arithmetic de Rham stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20203)
*Junhui Qin*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文定义了特征p域上概形X的算术de Rham叠X^{arith}，提供了刚性上同调及其系数（超收敛等晶体和算术D-模）的叠论方法，避免了以往方法中框架选择的难题，并证明了Berthelot关于光滑真推前保持超收敛等晶体的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，刚性上同调及其系数的构造依赖于框架（frame）的选择，这是长期存在的技术难题。作者希望引入叠论（stack-theoretic）框架来系统地、自然地处理刚性上同调及其系数，消除框架选择的任意性。

Method: 作者定义了特征p域上概形的算术de Rham叠X^{arith}，研究其与Hyodo-Kato叠等已知叠对象的关系，并利用该叠论框架统一处理刚性上同调、超收敛等晶体和算术D-模。

Result: 建立了算术de Rham叠的理论框架，证明其可给出刚性上同调及系数的叠论描述，并利用该形式化方法证明了Berthelot猜想——光滑真推前保持超收敛等晶体。

Conclusion: 算术de Rham叠为特征p同调理论提供了不依赖框架选择的自然叠论语言，不仅统一了刚性上同调及其系数的理论，还解决了长期悬而未决的Berthelot猜想，展现了该形式化的算术应用价值。

Abstract: We define and study the arithmetic de Rham stack $X^{\operatorname{arith}}$ of a scheme $X$ over a field of characteristic $p$, and analyze its relation with related stacks such as the Hyodo--Kato stack. We show that $X^{\operatorname{arith}}$ gives a stack-theoretic approach to rigid cohomology and its coefficients, known as overconvergent isocrystals and arithmetic $D$-modules, which avoids the long-standing problem of frame-choosing in earlier approaches. We finally give some arithmetic applications of our formalism, such as a proof of Berthelot's conjecture on the preservation of overconvergent isocrystals by smooth proper pushforward.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [Disproof of the Yau--Tian--Donaldson conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19301)
*Jihao Liu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个极化光滑射影五维簇，证明它满足K-多项稳定性（K-polystability）但不存在常标量曲率Kähler度量，从而否证了关于cscK度量的Yau-Tian-Donaldson猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yau-Tian-Donaldson猜想是Kähler几何中的核心猜想，宣称K-多项式稳定性与cscK度量的存在性等价。此前在光滑簇情形该猜想在低维已被证实，本文试图通过构造高维（五维）反例来检验该猜想的边界，为认识K-稳定性与cscK度量之间的深层关系提供新视角。

Method: 本文构造了一个特定极化的光滑射影五维簇，并通过验证其满足K-多项式稳定性条件，同时利用几何分析与障碍理论证明该簇不存在cscK度量。值得注意的是，主要结果借助了生成式人工智能（GPT-5.6-sol、Fable 5和Danus系统）获得，附录中与Bin Dong和Guoxiong Gao合著了关于AI使用的详细报告。

Result: 成功构造了一个光滑射影五维簇，该簇满足K-多项式稳定性但不具有cscK度量，构成Yau-Tian-Donaldson猜想（cscK版本）的反例。

Conclusion: 在光滑射影五维簇上，K-多项式稳定性并非cscK度量存在性的充分条件，Yau-Tian-Donaldson猜想在光滑高维情形下不成立。这标志着该猜想适用范围的重大修正，也展示了AI辅助数学研究在发现反例方面的潜力。

Abstract: We construct a polarized smooth projective fivefold and prove that it is K-polystable but does not admit a constant scalar curvature Kähler metric. This disproves the Yau--Tian--Donaldson conjecture for constant scalar curvature metrics.
  The main result of this paper was obtained using generative AI, particularly GPT-5.6-sol, Fable 5, and the Danus system. A detailed report on the use of generative AI in this paper is enclosed in the appendix, joint with Bin Dong and Guoxiong Gao.

</details>


### [151] [Weyl forms in parabolic contact geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19386)
*Toby Aldape*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在无挠正规抛物接触Cartan几何中，将Rho张量用Weyl联络显式表达，并利用Cap–Slovák的配方将所有抛物接触几何中的Weyl联络用Tanaka–Webster联络表示；作为应用，推导出可积Legendre接触几何中调和曲率分量的公式，给出与模型空间Flag_{1,n+1}(R^{n+2})局部同构的完全障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在抛物接触Cartan几何中，接触形式决定Weyl形式（包含焊接形式、Weyl联络和Rho张量三个分量）。焊接形式已有显式描述，但Rho张量与Weyl联络的关系尚未得到系统刻画。同时Tanaka–Webster联络比Weyl联络更易显式定义，需要建立两者之间的系统转换公式。

Method: 利用Cap–Slovák给出的将Weyl联络用Tanaka–Webster联络表达的简单配方，将其应用于所有无挠正规抛物接触几何（包括CR、可积Legendre接触、接触射影及所有平坦接触型几何），推导出Weyl联络的具体公式，并进一步导出Rho张量关于Weyl联络的表达式。

Result: 在所有无挠正规抛物接触几何中给出了Rho张量关于Weyl联络的显式表达，并在每一类抛物接触几何中给出了Weyl联络关于Tanaka–Webster联络的公式。此外，在可积Legendre接触几何中推导出调和曲率分量的公式，其为与模型空间Flag_{1,n+1}(R^{n+2})局部同构的完全障碍，恢复并复现了文献[16]的结果。

Conclusion: 本文完成了抛物接触几何中三个基本结构分量（焊接形式、Weyl联络、Rho张量）之间关系的系统刻画，统一推广了既有结果，并通过调和曲率的应用验证了这些公式的有效性。

Abstract: In a parabolic contact Cartan geometry a choice of contact form determines a Weyl form, an important piece of data for understanding the space from the perspective of Cartan geometry. The Weyl form decomposes into three components: a soldering form, Weyl connection and Rho tensor. An explicit description of the soldering form is well-known. We express the Rho tensor in terms of the Weyl connection in all torsion-free normal parabolic contact geometries. This includes CR, integrable Legendrian contact, and contact projective (vanishing contact torsion) geometries in addition to all flat geometries of contact type. Parabolic contact geometries with a specified contact form have a second canonical connection, the Tanaka--Webster connection, which is easier to define explicitly than the Weyl connection. In [7] \v Cap and Slovák give a simple recipe for expressing the Weyl connection in terms of the Tanaka--Webster connection. We apply this recipe to every parabolic contact geometry, giving a formula for the Weyl connection in terms of the Tanaka--Webster connection in each case. As an application of some of these results we derive formulas for the harmonic curvature components in integrable Legendrian contact geometry, which provide a complete obstruction to local isomorphism to the model space $\mathrm{Flag}_{1,n+1}(\R^{n+2})$, recovering a result in [16].

</details>


### [152] [On the holonomy of Lie algebroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19399)
*Pooja Joshi,Joel Villatoro*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文为Lie algebroid引入了和乐群胚（holonomy groupoid），该构造同时推广了叶状结构的和乐群胚与Lie代数的伴随表示。作者证明了该群胚是纵向光滑的，并计算了其Lie algebroid。当群胚的Lie algebroid与给定Lie algebroid一致时，该和乐群胚是典范的终端整合：每个源连通整合都唯一地诱导一个态射。构造中利用了Lie algebroid时变截面的"flow product"概念，为Weinstein群胚的结构提供了替代定义方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lie algebroid的整合（integration）是几何中的重要问题，Weinstein群胚是已知的源连通的整合构造，但缺乏一种更"典范"或终端性的构造。受叶状结构和乐群胚以及Lie代数伴随表示的启发，作者希望为Lie algebroid建立一种统一的和乐群胚构造，使其在特定条件下成为典范的终端整合。

Method: 作者定义Lie algebroid的和乐群胚，利用时变截面的"flow product"概念来构造群胚运算（乘法）。该构造通过推广叶状结构的和乐群胚（基于路径的横向和乐）和Lie代数的伴随表示（作为微小的和乐元）来实现。作者证明该群胚的纵向光滑性，计算其Lie algebroid，并验证当与给定algebroid一致时满足终端性质。

Result: 得到了Lie algebroid的和乐群胚，证明了其纵向光滑性，并计算了其Lie algebroid。在Lie algebroid与该群胚的algebroid一致的情况下，和乐群胚是典范的终端整合——任何源连通整合都唯一存在一个态射，该态射在无穷小层面诱导恒等映射。此外，flow product方法也为Weinstein群胚的群胚结构提供了新的构造途径。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了Lie代数的伴随表示、叶状结构的和乐群胚与Lie algebroid整合之间的统一框架。和乐群胚在合适条件下成为典范的终端整合，填补了Lie algebroid整合理论中的一个空缺，并给出了构造Weinstein群胚结构的替代方案。

Abstract: We introduce a holonomy groupoid for Lie algebroids. This construction generalizes both the holonomy groupoid of a foliation and the adjoint representation of a Lie algebra. We prove that the resulting groupoid is longitudinally smooth and compute its Lie algebroid. When the algebroid of the holonomy groupoid coincides with the given Lie algebroid, the holonomy groupoid is the canonical terminal integration: every source-connected integration admits a unique morphism into it inducing the identity infinitesimally. To construct the groupoid structure on the holonomy groupoid, we utilize the notion of the "flow product" of time-dependent sections of a Lie algebroid. This provides an alternative way to define the groupoid structure for the Weinstein groupoid.

</details>


### [153] [A problem for isolated singularities of surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19452)
*Flavio Mosca*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了黎曼曲面（作为R^n的子流形）的共形结构在孤立奇点处的延拓问题。主要结果（1989年）表明：当孤立奇点是M-正则时（满足Whitney条件且球面截线的长度至少线性衰减），该奇点是共形点状的（即共形等价于穿孔圆盘）。证明采用环模（极值长度）论证，将经典平面技巧推广到R^n中的子流形。文中还处理了两类例子：单曲线生成的旋转曲面和子解析曲面。最后给出了一个光滑反例，证明严格的Whitney条件本身不蕴含线性长度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典平面情形下，黎曼曲面的孤立奇点可通过极值长度（模）方法判定是否"共形点状"。作者希望将此理论从平面推广到高维欧氏空间中的子流形，并厘清"度量条件"（而非解析条件）在判定共形延拓中的充分性和必要性。关键在于找到合适的度量条件，使奇点处曲面的共形结构可以被完全刻画。

Method: (1) 提出M-正则性的定义：包含Whitney条件（在奇点处切锥有良好行为）和"度量衰减"条件（球面截线长度至少线性下降）；(2) 利用环模/极值长度论证，将经典的平面模方法推广至高维子流形，证明M-正则蕴含共形点状性；(3) 对旋转曲面和子解析曲面两类例子直接验证M-正则性（后者借助Hironaka结构理论）；(4) 构造C^∞反例，证明两类条件独立。

Result: 主要定理：R^n中孤立奇点是M-正则的，则该奇点是共形点状的。推论：可定向子解析曲面的每个孤立奇点都是共形点状的。同时构造了C^∞反例，表明仅满足严格Whitney条件而长度界不成立时，奇点不一定是共形点状的，从而证明M-正则性中的两个假设是独立且各自必要的。

Conclusion: M-正则性提供了判定孤立奇点共形点状的充分条件，其中Whitney条件和线性长度衰减缺一不可。这一工作将平面经典理论推广至高维子流形，并通过子解析曲面的情形展示了度量方法与代数几何结构理论的结合。反例的构造则精确刻画了该条件的边界。

Abstract: We study the extension of the conformal structure of a Riemann surface, obtained as a submanifold of $\mathbf R^n$, across an isolated singular point, under hypotheses that are metric rather than analytic. The main result (1989) is that an isolated singularity is $\textbf{conformally point-like}$ (conformal to a punctured disc) whenever it is $\textbf{$M$-regular}$: the pair (surface minus the point, the point) satisfies a Whitney condition, and the length of the spherical slice $S(0,r)\cap E$ decreases at most linearly in $r$, a condition described as ``metric decay''. This is proved via a modulus-of-rings (extremal-length) argument, generalizing the classical planar technique to submanifolds of $\mathbf R^n$. Two classes of examples are treated: surfaces of revolution generated by a single curve, and subanalytic surfaces, for which $M$-regularity is verified directly from Hironaka's structure theory, giving as a corollary that every isolated singularity of an orientable subanalytic surface is conformally point-like. A further original result is a $C^\infty$ counterexample showing that a strict Whitney condition alone does $\textbf{not}$ imply the linear length bound: the two hypotheses in the definition of $M$-regularity are independent.

</details>


### [154] [Polar and torus manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19470)
*Francisco C. Caramello,Dirk Toeben*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了拟环流形（quasitoric manifolds）可以配备不变度量，使其环面作用成为极作用（polar action）。更一般地，环面的无穷小极作用存在不变黎曼度量使之成为极作用，当且仅当其商空间的实轨形欧拉类为零。由于拟环流形的轨道空间是可缩的简单多胞形，该上同调障碍平凡地消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究拟环流形上的环面等距极作用的存在性问题，将极作用的度量存在性与商空间的拓扑（实轨形欧拉类）联系起来，给出一个完整的充要条件。
